        parent = parent.parentElement;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = row.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="default-thinking-behaviour"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#default-thinking-behaviour" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer"><strong>Default Thinking Behaviour</strong></span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Thinking is activated by default in GLM-5.3 GLM-5.3-FLASH GLM-5.2 GLM-5.1 GLM-5 GLM-4.7 series, different from the default hybrid thinking in GLM-4.6.</span>
<blockquote>
<span data-as="p">If you want to disable thinking, use the following (note that <code>GLM-5.3</code> and <code>GLM-5.3-FLASH</code> use forced thinking and cannot be disabled):</span>
</blockquote>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="interleaved-thinking"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#interleaved-thinking" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer"><strong>Interleaved thinking</strong></span></h2>
<span data-as="p">We support <strong>interleaved thinking</strong> by default, allowing GLM to think between tool calls and after receiving tool results. This enables more complex, step-by-step reasoning: interpreting each tool output before deciding what to do next, chaining multiple tool calls with reasoning steps, and making finer-grained decisions based on intermediate results.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Tip" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-green-200 bg-green-50 dark:border-green-900 dark:bg-green-600/20 dark:text-green-300"><path d="M 4.0216 15.9727 C 4.0216 16.1941 4.0855 16.4101 4.2074 16.5947 L 4.81 17.4979 C 4.9946 17.7747 5.4137 18 5.7467 18 H 7.9149 C 8.2468 18 8.6659 17.775 8.8505 17.4979 L 9.451 16.5951 C 9.5543 16.439 9.6391 16.1601 9.6391 15.9727 L 9.6436 14.5945 H 4.0181 L 4.0216 15.9727 Z M 6.8308 0 C 3.2453 0.0113 0.6429 2.9171 0.6429 6.1563 C 0.6429 7.7167 1.2209 9.1392 2.1744 10.2278 C 2.7556 10.8901 3.663 12.2751 4.0104 13.443 C 4.0115 13.4518 4.0137 13.4612 4.0148 13.4706 H 9.6473 C 9.6483 13.4612 9.6505 13.4524 9.6517 13.443 C 9.9988 12.2751 10.9065 10.8901 11.4877 10.2278 C 12.4422 9.1695 13.0189 7.749 13.0189 6.1563 C 13.0189 2.7705 10.2483 0.0001 6.8308 0 Z M 10.2202 9.1449 C 9.6696 9.7724 8.9882 10.7727 8.4956 11.8131 H 5.1692 C 4.6766 10.7727 3.9953 9.7724 3.445 9.1452 C 2.7257 8.3257 2.3305 7.2463 2.3305 6.1563 C 2.3305 3.9835 4.0216 1.6964 6.7992 1.6876 C 9.3131 1.6876 11.3312 3.7058 11.3312 6.1563 C 11.3312 7.2463 10.9374 8.3257 10.2202 9.1449 Z M 6.2683 2.8127 C 4.7178 2.8127 3.4556 4.0749 3.4556 5.6254 C 3.4556 5.9364 3.7072 6.188 4.0181 6.188 C 4.3291 6.188 4.5807 5.9348 4.5807 5.6254 C 4.5807 4.6948 5.3376 3.9378 6.2683 3.9378 C 6.5792 3.9378 6.8308 3.6865 6.8308 3.3756 C 6.8308 3.0647 6.5777 2.8127 6.2683 2.8127 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">When using interleaved thinking with tools, <strong>thinking blocks should be explicitly preserved and returned together with the tool results.</strong></span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The detailed interleaved thinking process is as follows.</span>
<span data-as="p"><span data-ramka-trigger="" class="zoom-image-trigger contents cursor-zoom-in"><span class="contents"><img <h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="preserved-thinking"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#preserved-thinking" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer"><strong>Preserved thinking</strong></span></h2>
<span data-as="p"><strong>We introduce a new capability</strong> in coding scenarios: the model can retain <strong>reasoning content from previous assistant turns</strong> in the context. This helps preserve reasoning continuity and conversation integrity, improves model performance, and increases cache hit rates—saving tokens in real tasks.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Check" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-green-200 bg-green-50 dark:border-green-900 dark:bg-green-600/20" data-callout-type="check"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-green-800 dark:text-green-300"><path d="M 15.4195 3.7055 C 15.859 4.1449 15.859 4.8551 15.4195 5.2945 L 6.4195 14.2945 C 5.9801 14.734 5.2699 14.734 4.8305 14.2945 L 0.3295 9.7945 C -0.1098 9.3551 -0.1098 8.6449 0.3295 8.2055 C 0.7689 7.766 1.4811 7.766 1.9206 8.2055 L 5.5934 11.9074 L 13.8305 3.7055 C 14.2699 3.2653 14.9801 3.2653 15.4195 3.7055 H 15.4195 z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">This capability is <strong>enabled by default</strong> on the <strong>Coding Plan endpoint</strong> and <strong>disabled by default</strong> on the <strong>standard API endpoint</strong>. If you want to enable <strong>Preserved Thinking</strong> in your product (primarily recommended for coding/agent scenarios), you can turn it on for the API endpoint by setting <strong>“clear_thinking”: false</strong>, and <strong>you must return the complete</strong>, unmodified reasoning_content back to the API.</span><span data-as="p">All consecutive reasoning_content blocks must <strong>exactly match the original sequence</strong> generated by the model during the initial request. Do not reorder or edit these blocks; otherwise, performance may degrade and cache hit rates may be affected.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The detailed Preserved thinking process is as follows.</span>
<span data-as="p"><span data-ramka-trigger="" class="zoom-image-trigger contents cursor-zoom-in"><span class="contents"><img transition-[background-color,box-shadow,scale,opacity,color] duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-white/65 active:scale-[0.96] active:bg-white/75 focus:outline-hidden focus-visible:shadow-[0_0_0_2px_rgb(0_0_0/0.25),0_0.5px_1px_rgb(0_0_0/0.12),0_1px_2px_rgb(0_0_0/0.08),inset_0_0.5px_0_rgb(255_255_255/0.65)] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30 disabled:active:scale-100 dark:border-white/25 dark:bg-neutral-900/45 dark:text-white <h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="turn-level-thinking"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#turn-level-thinking" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Turn-level Thinking</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">“Turn-level Thinking” is a capability that <strong>lets you control reasoning computation on a per-turn basis</strong>: within the same session, each request can independently choose to enable or disable thinking. This is a new capability introduced in GLM-4.7, with the following advantages:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>More flexible cost/latency control:</strong> For lightweight turns like “asking a fact” or “tweaking wording,” you can disable thinking to get faster responses; for heavier tasks like “complex planning,” “multi-constraint reasoning,” or “code debugging,” you can enable thinking to improve accuracy and stability.</li>
<li><strong>Smoother multi-turn experience:</strong> The thinking switch can be toggled at any point within a session. The model stays coherent across turns and keeps a consistent output style, making it feel “smarter when things are hard, faster when things are simple.”</li>
<li><strong>Better for agent/tool-use scenarios:</strong> On turns that require quick tool execution, you can reduce reasoning overhead; on turns that require making decisions based on tool results, you can turn on deeper thinking—dynamically balancing efficiency and quality.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="example-usage"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#example-usage" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Example Usage</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">This applies to both <strong>Interleaved Thinking</strong> and <strong>Preserved Thinking</strong>—no manual differentiation is required. <strong>Remember to return the historical</strong> <code>reasoning_content</code><strong>to keep the reasoning coherent.</strong></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955"># Round 1: the model reasons and then calls a tool</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;type&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;enabled&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;clear_thinking&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">False</span><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">  # False for Preserved Thinking</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    }})</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">reasoning, content, tool_calls </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">=</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, []</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">for</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> chunk </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">in</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> response:</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        content </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">+=</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> delta.content</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">print</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">f</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Reasoning: </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">{</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">reasoning</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D7BA7D">\n</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">Reply: </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">{</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">content</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">)</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true"
